1	import * as ed from "@noble/ed25519";
2	import type { Ed25519Scheme, VerifyFn } from "../../provider/webhook";
3	
4	function _base64ToUint8Array(b64: string): Uint8Array {
5	  return Uint8Array.from(atob(b64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
6	}
7	
8	export function _deriveEd25519Verify(scheme: Ed25519Scheme): VerifyFn {
9	  return async (rawBody, headers, secret) => {
10	    const rawSig = headers.get(scheme.signatureHeader);
11	    if (!rawSig) {
12	      return false;
13	    }
14	    // Svix sends space-separated base64 signatures; any must match
15	    const signatures = scheme.multiSignature ? rawSig.split(" ") : [rawSig];
16	    const secretBytes = _base64ToUint8Array(secret);
17	    const messageBytes = scheme.timestampHeader
18	      ? new TextEncoder().encode(
19	          `${headers.get(scheme.timestampHeader)}.${rawBody}`
20	        )
21	      : new TextEncoder().encode(rawBody);
22	    for (const sig of signatures) {
23	      const sigBytes = _base64ToUint8Array(sig);
24	      if (await ed.verifyAsync(sigBytes, messageBytes, secretBytes)) {
25	        return true;
26	      }
27	    }
28	    return false;
29	  };
30	}
31	
```

**Phase 3 Complete - Ready for Manual Verification**

Automated verification passed:
- `pnpm install` succeeds (9 packages added, 12 removed; `uuid@13.0.0` resolves in `core/ai-sdk` + `vendor/lib`)
- `SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION=true pnpm typecheck` passes (53 tasks)
- `SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION=true pnpm test` passes (12 tasks)
- `pnpm build:app`, `pnpm build:platform`, `pnpm build:www` all succeed
- `pnpm list uuid -r --depth 0` confirms `uuid@13.0.0` everywhere

Edits made:
- `uuid ^11.1.0 → ^13.0.0` and `@types/uuid ^10.0.0 → ^11.0.0` in `vendor/lib/package.json` + `core/ai-sdk/package.json`
- `@noble/ed25519: ^2.2.3 → ^3.1.0` in `pnpm-workspace.yaml` catalog
- `jiti ^1.21.6 → ^2.6.1` in `apps/www/package.json`
- `dotenv-cli: ^11.0.0` added to catalog; all 7 consumers (`apps/app`, `apps/platform`, `apps/www`, `db/app`, `vendor/db`, `packages/app-test-data`, `packages/webhook-schemas`) switched to `"catalog:"`

Implementation finding logged in the plan: `@types/uuid@11` is still deprecated (uuid now ships its own types). Typecheck clean, but a follow-up could remove `@types/uuid` entirely.

Please perform the manual verification steps listed in the plan:
- `cd apps/app && pnpm with-env pnpm exec echo OK` prints `OK` on a clean line (dotenv-cli 11)
- Trigger a webhook signature verify through `packages/app-providers/src/runtime/verify/ed25519.ts` (Svix webhook) — confirm verification succeeds
- `apps/www` dev server boots (jiti 2 loader works for env)
- Any route calling uuid.v4 returns a valid UUID

Let me know when manual testing is complete so I can proceed to Phase 4.

1	# Collapse `@vendor/lib` to `nanoid`-only (Phase D.2) Implementation Plan
2	
3	## Overview
4	
5	Delete the four zero-consumer modules in `@vendor/lib` (`errors.ts`, `uuid.ts`, `datetime/`, `pretty-project-name.ts`) and reduce the package to a thin `nanoid` vendor shim — its only load-bearing export. Insert this deletion as **new Phase D.2** in the active Phase D plan (`thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-core-packages-upgrade-phase-d.md`), renumbering existing D.2–D.8 upward by one and trimming the (new) D.4 uuid-bump scope that targeted `vendor/lib/package.json`. Mirrors the `joyful`/`friendly-words.ts` deletion pattern already shipped in D.1 (commit `ec5dcf5ef`), extended across the full zero-consumer surface.
6	
7	## Current State Analysis
8	
9	`@vendor/lib` is the renamed successor of the former `@repo/lib` grab-bag. Direct file reads + repo-wide grep (research: `thoughts/shared/research/2026-04-19-vendor-lib-errors-usage.md`) confirm the import-boundary state:
10	
11	**Load-bearing (keep):**
12	
13	- `vendor/lib/src/nanoid.ts` (7 lines) — 16 source importers, all via `from "@vendor/lib"` root barrel. See `api/platform/src/lib/oauth/authorize.ts:9`, `vendor/observability/src/trpc.ts:13`, `packages/app-api-key/src/crypto.ts:11`, + 13 `db/app/src/schema/tables/*.ts` files.
14	
15	**Zero-consumer (delete):**
16	
17	- `vendor/lib/src/errors.ts` (64 lines) — defines `DomainError`, `DomainErrorOptions`, `isDomainError`. Repo-wide grep returns matches only inside `errors.ts` itself, `index.ts:2-3` (re-export), and `thoughts/` docs.
18	- `vendor/lib/src/uuid.ts` (3 lines) — wraps `uuid.v4` as `uuidv4`. The identifier `uuidv4` does appear in `core/ai-sdk/src/core/server/runtime.ts:8` and README — **but both via `import { v4 as uuidv4 } from "uuid"` direct import**, not through `@vendor/lib`. The `@vendor/lib`-exported `uuidv4` has zero importers.
19	- `vendor/lib/src/datetime/index.ts` (3 lines) — exports `formatMySqlDateTime`. Grep for `formatMySqlDateTime` returns only its definition + the `index.ts:1` re-export.
20	- `vendor/lib/src/pretty-project-name.ts` (200 lines) — exports `generatePrettyProjectName`. Grep returns only the definition line; no imports of `@vendor/lib/pretty-project-name` anywhere.
21	
22	**Wiring:**
23	
24	- `vendor/lib/src/index.ts` (5 lines) currently re-exports `formatMySqlDateTime`, `DomainError`/`isDomainError`/`DomainErrorOptions`, `nanoid`, `uuidv4`. Four of five lines are dead.
25	- `vendor/lib/package.json:7-28` declares four subpath exports (`./pretty-project-name`, `./datetime`, `./uuid`, `./nanoid`) plus the root `.`. Zero importers use any subpath (all 16 consumers use the root barrel).
26	- `vendor/lib/package.json` runtime deps: `nanoid` (catalog) + `uuid ^11.1.0`. Only `nanoid` is load-bearing after deletion; `uuid` disappears with `uuid.ts`.
27	- `vendor/lib/package.json` devDeps: `@types/uuid ^10.0.0` — load-bearing only for `uuid.ts`; disappears with it.
28	- No test file exists for any of the four dead modules (`vendor/lib/src/**/*.test.ts` glob is empty).
29	
30	**Interaction with in-flight Phase D work:**
31	
32	- Phase D.1 (commit `ec5dcf5ef`) — already merged the analogous `joyful`/`friendly-words.ts` deletion pattern. Done.
33	- Phase D.3 ("API-Change Majors" in the current plan, which becomes **D.4** after this insertion) is planned to bump `uuid 11→13` and `@types/uuid 10→11` in **both** `vendor/lib/package.json` (lines 42, 48) and `core/ai-sdk/package.json` (lines 81, 88). Inserting this collapse as D.2 drops those deps from `vendor/lib/package.json` entirely — so the (new) D.4 uuid section must be trimmed to only edit `core/ai-sdk/package.json`.
34	
35	**Stale artifact:**
36	
37	- `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-delete-vendor-lib-errors.md` — narrow plan from a prior session covering only the `DomainError` trio. Superseded by this broader plan; delete as part of implementation.
38	
39	## Desired End State
40	
41	- `vendor/lib/src/` contains only `index.ts` + `nanoid.ts`.
42	- `vendor/lib/src/index.ts` is a single-line barrel: `export { nanoid } from "./nanoid";`.
43	- `vendor/lib/package.json` exports map has a single `.` entry (no subpaths); runtime deps narrow to `nanoid` only; `uuid` + `@types/uuid` removed.
44	- `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-core-packages-upgrade-phase-d.md` has a new Phase 2 section for this collapse; existing Phase 2–8 renumbered to 3–9; the (new) Phase 4 uuid-bump section no longer references `vendor/lib/package.json`.
45	- `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-delete-vendor-lib-errors.md` is removed (superseded).
46	- `pnpm install && SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION=true pnpm typecheck && SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION=true pnpm test && pnpm knip --no-exit-code && pnpm build:app && pnpm build:platform && pnpm build:www` all pass.
47	- `pnpm-lock.yaml` diff shows `uuid@11.1.0` and `@types/uuid@10.0.0` dropped from the `vendor/lib` subgraph; no other dep changes.
48	- All 16 existing `from "@vendor/lib"` importers of `nanoid` continue to resolve and build without source edits.
49	
50	### Key Discoveries
51	
52	- `vendor/lib/src/errors.ts:1-64` — entire surface to delete; self-contained, only extends built-in `Error`.
53	- `vendor/lib/src/uuid.ts:1-3` — thin wrapper `import { v4 as uuidv4 } from "uuid"; export { uuidv4 };`. Deletion drops the `uuid` + `@types/uuid` deps automatically.
54	- `vendor/lib/src/datetime/index.ts:1-3` — single 1-line helper; leaf dir will be empty after removal, so delete the `datetime/` directory itself.
55	- `vendor/lib/src/pretty-project-name.ts:1-200` — entire file; imports `./nanoid` internally, so deletion does not orphan `nanoid.ts`.
56	- `vendor/lib/src/index.ts:1-5` — current barrel; collapses to 1 load-bearing line.
57	- `vendor/lib/package.json:7-28` — exports map; collapses to 4 lines (just the `.` entry). `package.json:35-44` deps/devDeps — `uuid` + `@types/uuid` removed.
58	- `vendor/lib/src/pretty-project-name.ts:49` — `import { nanoid } from "./nanoid"` is *internal*; removing `pretty-project-name.ts` does not affect external consumers of `nanoid`.
59	- All 16 `from "@vendor/lib"` consumers in the main tree import `{ nanoid }` only — verified by full-tree grep. Zero edits needed outside `vendor/lib/`.
60	- Phase D.1 precedent — commit `ec5dcf5ef` deleted `vendor/lib/src/friendly-words.ts` + its package.json subpath export + its `joyful` dep using the same pattern. This plan applies the same pattern to four more modules in one commit.
61	
62	## What We're NOT Doing
63	
64	- **Removing `nanoid`.** 16 load-bearing consumers; it is the entire reason `@vendor/lib` exists after this collapse.
65	- **Renaming or relocating `@vendor/lib`.** The package name, directory, and `vendor` tag stay. Future work can consider re-homing `nanoid` into its own vendor package if useful, but not in this plan.
66	- **Providing replacements for the deleted modules.** No consumer is asking for `DomainError`, `formatMySqlDateTime`, `uuidv4` (via `@vendor/lib`), or `generatePrettyProjectName`. They are deleted, not relocated. If a future feature needs any of them, it can be added at that time co-located with its consumer.
67	- **Editing any of the 16 `@vendor/lib` consumer files.** They import `{ nanoid }` and will continue to.
68	- **Changing `core/ai-sdk/src/core/server/runtime.ts`.** It uses `import { v4 as uuidv4 } from "uuid"` directly — unchanged by this plan.
69	- **Adding tests.** No tests exist for the deleted modules; no new tests are warranted for deletion.
70	- **CI / knip wiring.** Out of scope; Phase D explicitly declared knip a manual check.
71	- **Adding subpath exports to the new minimal `vendor/lib/package.json`.** The existing 16 consumers all use the root barrel. A `./nanoid` subpath has zero users; drop it along with the rest.
72	
73	## Implementation Approach
74	
75	Single commit, single revert unit. Six source-file operations + one `package.json` edit + two planning-doc edits:
76	
77	1. Delete `vendor/lib/src/errors.ts`.
78	2. Delete `vendor/lib/src/uuid.ts`.
79	3. Delete `vendor/lib/src/datetime/index.ts` + the now-empty `vendor/lib/src/datetime/` directory.
80	4. Delete `vendor/lib/src/pretty-project-name.ts`.
81	5. Collapse `vendor/lib/src/index.ts` to a single `nanoid` re-export.
82	6. Trim `vendor/lib/package.json` — drop 3 subpath exports, drop `uuid` + `@types/uuid`.
83	7. Edit `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-core-packages-upgrade-phase-d.md` — insert new Phase 2, renumber Phase 2–8 → 3–9, trim the new Phase 4 uuid scope.
84	8. Delete the superseded narrow plan file `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-delete-vendor-lib-errors.md`.
85	
86	Zero consumer edits. The verification suite is a defensive sanity pass, not a diagnostic surface — typecheck across 52 turbo tasks catches any accidentally-imported symbol.
87

347	- [ ] `pnpm install` succeeds with no lockfile conflicts
348	- [ ] `pnpm-lock.yaml` diff: `uuid@11.1.0` and `@types/uuid@10.0.0` disappear from the `vendor/lib` subgraph; all other deps unchanged

351	- [ ] `pnpm knip --no-exit-code` reports no new findings vs Phase D baseline; `unresolved imports` count unchanged

355	- [ ] `pnpm build:www` succeeds

357	- [ ] Repo-wide grep (`rg -t ts -t tsx 'from ["\x27]@vendor/lib/(pretty-project-name|datetime|uuid|nanoid)["\x27]' apps api db packages vendor core`) returns zero hits (confirms no code used the subpaths we're removing)
358	
359	#### Manual Verification:
360	
361	- [ ] `pnpm dev:full` boots; `/tmp/console-dev.log` shows no new warnings or module-resolution errors
362	- [ ] `http://localhost:3024` (app), `http://localhost:3024/docs` (www via microfrontends), `http://localhost:4112` (platform direct) all render without console errors
363	- [ ] Exercise one `nanoid` code path (e.g. create an API key via `packages/app-api-key/src/crypto.ts:11` or hit an endpoint that generates an id via one of the `db/app/src/schema/tables/*.ts` default generators) — confirm unchanged behaviour
364	- [ ] Skim the renumbered Phase D plan top-to-bottom — confirm no stale `Phase N` references outside the Improvement Log
365	
366	**Implementation Note**: After automated verification passes, pause for human confirmation of the manual verification before opening the PR. Commit message should reference the Phase D position explicitly, e.g. `chore(deps): phase D.2 — collapse @vendor/lib to nanoid-only`.
367	
368	---
369	
370	## Testing Strategy
371	
372	### Unit Tests
373	
374	Nothing to add. The deleted surface has no tests (`vendor/lib/src/**/*.test.ts` glob empty), and the one remaining module (`nanoid.ts`, 7 lines) is a thin wrapper around `customAlphabet` whose behaviour is already exercised implicitly by the 16 consumers.
375	
376	### Integration Tests
377	
378	The full `pnpm typecheck` across 52 turbo tasks is the integration surface. Any in-tree consumer that imported one of the four deleted symbols (none do per grep, but belt-and-braces) would fail at compile time.
379	
380	The `pnpm build:app`, `pnpm build:platform`, `pnpm build:www` trio catches the bundler-level resolution of `from "@vendor/lib"` imports across all app surfaces.
381	
382	### Manual Testing Steps
383	
384	1. Branch from the current Phase D branch: work continues on `chore/core-packages-upgrade-phase-d` (no new branch).
385	2. Apply the eight file operations in order (six file-ops in `vendor/lib/` + Phase D plan edits + stale-plan deletion).
386	3. Run the full automated verification list.
387	4. Boot `pnpm dev:full`; load the three URLs; confirm no regressions.
388	5. Exercise one `nanoid()` code path end-to-end.
389	6. Commit as `chore(deps): phase D.2 — collapse @vendor/lib to nanoid-only`.
390	7. Continue with Phase D.3 (ex-D.2, "Drop-in CLI/Type Majors") on the same branch.
391	
392	## Performance Considerations
393	
394	None. Deleting 270 lines of source across four unused modules has no runtime impact. Bundle size in production builds should be unchanged (tree-shaking already dropped these symbols for every consumer). TypeScript server memory drops marginally (`vendor/lib` typecheck graph is two modules instead of six). `pnpm install` becomes ~2 MB lighter on disk (one fewer `uuid` package tree).
395	
396	## Migration Notes
397	
398	No data migration. No schema change. Rollback is `git revert <commit>` + `pnpm install` — the dep removal reverses cleanly.
399	
400	If a future feature needs `DomainError`, `formatMySqlDateTime`, `uuidv4` (via a shared vendor path), or `generatePrettyProjectName`, it should be added at that time, co-located with its first consumer or promoted to a dedicated package — rather than revived from this deletion. The git history and this plan document the original surface if reference is needed.
401	
402	## References
403	
404	- Research: `thoughts/shared/research/2026-04-19-vendor-lib-errors-usage.md` — usage audit that established the `DomainError` zero-consumer claim; line 99 extends the zero-consumer list to the remaining four surfaces.
405	- Phase D plan (to be edited): `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-core-packages-upgrade-phase-d.md`. Line anchors referenced above were accurate at time of writing; re-verify before editing if the plan has been touched since.
406	- Phase D.1 precedent: commit `ec5dcf5ef chore(deps): phase D.1 — hygiene bumps + joyful deletion` — direct analog for this plan's pattern applied to the `joyful`/`friendly-words.ts` surface.
407	- Superseded narrow plan (to be deleted): `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-delete-vendor-lib-errors.md`.
408	- Original migration context: `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-fix-ci-turbo-boundaries-vendor-lib.md` — documents the `@repo/lib` → `@vendor/lib` rehoming that carried all four dead modules forward verbatim; line 422 confirms the zero-consumer count for the full set.
409	- Files touched:
410	  - `vendor/lib/src/errors.ts` — delete.
411	  - `vendor/lib/src/uuid.ts` — delete.
412	  - `vendor/lib/src/datetime/index.ts` — delete; remove empty parent dir.
413	  - `vendor/lib/src/pretty-project-name.ts` — delete.
414	  - `vendor/lib/src/index.ts` — collapse to 1 line.
415	  - `vendor/lib/package.json` — trim exports map + drop `uuid` / `@types/uuid` deps.
416	  - `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-core-packages-upgrade-phase-d.md` — insert new Phase 2, renumber Phase 2→9, trim (new) Phase 4 uuid scope.
417	  - `thoughts/shared/plans/2026-04-19-delete-vendor-lib-errors.md` — delete.

287	## Phase 4: API-Change Majors
288	
289	### Overview
290	
291	Four packages across three sub-bumps with small but real migration work. All sub-bumps land in the same commit; if one regresses, the phase commit reverts the whole batch.
292	
293	### Changes Required:
294	
295	#### 1. `uuid 11 → 13` + `@types/uuid 10 → 11`
296	
297	**File**: `core/ai-sdk/package.json`
298	**Changes**: Lines 81, 88.
299	
300	```jsonc
301	"uuid": "^13.0.0",
302	"@types/uuid": "^11.0.0"
303	```
304	
305	Call site (`core/ai-sdk/src/core/server/runtime.ts:8`) uses a standard named import — no code change needed. Runtime is Node 22+, browser WebCrypto path defaults are fine. `vendor/lib` no longer declares `uuid` after Phase 2.
306	
307	#### 2. `@noble/ed25519 2 → 3`
308	
309	**File**: `pnpm-workspace.yaml`
310	**Changes**: Line 18 — catalog entry.
311	
312	```yaml
313	# catalog:
314	  '@noble/ed25519': ^3.1.0   # was ^2.2.3 — requires Uint8Array inputs, ed.hashes.* instead of ed.etc.sha512*
315	```
316	
317	Single consumer: `packages/app-providers/src/runtime/verify/ed25519.ts:16-24`. All inputs are already `Uint8Array` (line 16 `_base64ToUint8Array`, line 18-21 `TextEncoder().encode`, line 23 `_base64ToUint8Array`). The `ed.verifyAsync(sigBytes, messageBytes, secretBytes)` signature is unchanged between 2.x and 3.x. No `ed.etc.sha512*` or hash injection pattern in use. Zero source-code changes.
318	
319	#### 3. `jiti 1 → 2` (apps/www only)
320	
321	**File**: `apps/www/package.json`
322	**Changes**: Line 79.
323	
324	```jsonc
325	"jiti": "^2.6.1"   // was ^1.21.6
326	```
327	
328	`jiti` is declared as a dev dep but has zero source imports (confirmed at `knip.json:16` — it's in `ignoreDependencies` for apps/www). It's consumed implicitly as a transitive loader. The v2 breaking changes (default export removed, `jiti.resolve` → `jiti.esmResolve`, `experimentalBun` → `tryNative`) matter only if the package is imported explicitly. Bumping is safe.
329	
330	If a transitive consumer breaks, the lockfile will surface it — watch for errors during `pnpm install`.
331	
332	#### 4. `dotenv-cli 8 → 11` (+ promote to catalog)
333	
334	Seven workspace packages declare `dotenv-cli` directly as a devDep — `apps/app`, `apps/platform`, `apps/www`, `db/app`, `packages/app-test-data`, `packages/webhook-schemas`, `vendor/db`. Since we're editing every one of them anyway, promote to `catalog:` to prevent future drift.
335	
336	**File**: `pnpm-workspace.yaml`
337	**Changes**: Add a new catalog entry alongside other dev tools.
338	
339	```yaml
340	# catalog: (insert alphabetically)
341	  dotenv-cli: ^11.0.0
342	```
343	
344	**Files**: 7 package.json files listed above.
345	**Changes**: Replace the existing version string with `"catalog:"`.
346	
347	```jsonc
348	"dotenv-cli": "catalog:"   // was ^8.0.0 — promoted to catalog in this phase
349	```
350	
351	Used only as the script prefix in the `with-env` pattern. v9 changed the default to quiet (no startup output); v10 changed variable-expansion ordering in `.env`. `.env` files in this repo are flat `KEY=value` lines (verified: zero `${VAR}` expansion hits across the workspace); no ordering-change exposure. Verify: `cd apps/app && pnpm with-env pnpm exec echo OK` — should print `OK` without extra dotenv logs.
352	
353	### Success Criteria:
354	
355	#### Automated Verification:
356	
357	- [x] `pnpm install` succeeds
358	- [x] `SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION=true pnpm typecheck` passes
359	- [x] `SKIP_ENV_VALIDATION=true pnpm test` passes (watch the ai-sdk runtime test — uses `uuid`)
360	- [x] `pnpm build:app`, `pnpm build:platform`, `pnpm build:www` all succeed
361	- [x] `pnpm list uuid -r --depth 0` shows `uuid@13.x` for `core/ai-sdk` with no leftover 11.x from transitive (`vendor/lib` no longer declares uuid after Phase 2)
362	
363	#### Manual Verification:
364	
365	- [ ] `cd apps/app && pnpm with-env pnpm exec echo OK` prints `OK` on a clean line (dotenv-cli 11)
366	- [ ] Trigger a webhook signature verify that goes through `packages/app-providers/src/runtime/verify/ed25519.ts` (Svix webhook or similar) — confirm verification succeeds
367	- [ ] `apps/www` dev server boots (jiti 2 loader works for env)
368	- [ ] Any route that calls `crypto.randomUUID` equivalent via `uuid.v4` returns a valid UUID
369	
370	**Implementation Note**: After Phase 4 passes, commit and pause for human confirmation before Phase 5.
371	
372	**2026-04-19 implementation finding:** The `@types/uuid 10 → 11` bump did not clear the deprecation warning — `@types/uuid@11.0.0` is itself marked deprecated because `uuid@10+` ships its own type definitions. The stub still typechecks cleanly; to actually clear the warning, a follow-up should delete `@types/uuid` entirely from `vendor/lib/package.json` and `core/ai-sdk/package.json`. Left in place for now to keep Phase 3 scoped. No other issues: `uuid@13.0.0` resolves in both workspaces, the ed25519 verify call typechecks against `@noble/ed25519@3.x`, jiti 2 bump caused no transitive breakage, and `dotenv-cli` catalog promotion flowed through all 7 consumers.
373	
374	---
375	
376	## Phase 5: Remove Dead IITM/RITM Pins

83	  ["sql", langSql],
84	  ["typescript", langTs],
85	  ["ts", langTs],
86	  ["tsx", langTsx],
87	  ["yaml", langYaml],
88	  ["yml", langYaml],
89	]);
90	
91	class HighlighterManager {
92	  private highlighter: HighlighterCore | null = null;
93	  private readonly loadedLanguages = new Set<string>();
94	  private initializationPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
95	
96	  private async ensureHighlighterInitialized(
97	    language: BundledLanguage
98	  ): Promise<void> {
99	    const jsEngine = createJavaScriptRegexEngine({ forgiving: true });
100	
101	    if (!this.highlighter) {
102	      this.highlighter = await createHighlighterCore({
103	        themes: [githubLight, githubDark],
104	        langs: [],
105	        engine: jsEngine,
106	      });
107	    }
108	
109	    if (!this.loadedLanguages.has(language)) {
110	      const lang = LANG_REGISTRY.get(language);
111	      if (lang) {
112	        await this.highlighter.loadLanguage(
113	          lang as Parameters<HighlighterCore["loadLanguage"]>[0]
114	        );
115	      }
116	      this.loadedLanguages.add(language);
117	    }
118	  }
119	
120	  async highlightCode(
121	    code: string,
122	    language: BundledLanguage,
123	    themes: [BundledTheme, BundledTheme],
124	    preClassName?: string
125	  ): Promise<[string, string]> {
126	    // Ensure only one initialization happens at a time
127	    if (this.initializationPromise) {
128	      await this.initializationPromise;
129	    }
130	
131	    this.initializationPromise = this.ensureHighlighterInitialized(language);
132	    await this.initializationPromise;
133	    this.initializationPromise = null;
134	
135	    const addPreClass = (html: string) => {
136	      if (!preClassName) {
137	        return html;
138	      }
139	      return html.replace(PRE_TAG_REGEX, `<pre class="${preClassName}"$1`);
140	    };
141	
142	    const [lightTheme, darkTheme] = themes;
143	
144	    try {
145	      const light =
146	        this.highlighter?.codeToHtml(code, {
147	          lang: language,
148	          theme: lightTheme,
149	        }) ?? "";
150	      const dark =
151	        this.highlighter?.codeToHtml(code, {
152	          lang: language,
153	          theme: darkTheme,
154	        }) ?? "";
155	      return [
156	        light ? removePreBackground(addPreClass(light)) : "",
157	        dark ? removePreBackground(addPreClass(dark)) : "",
158	      ];
159	    } catch {
160	      // Language not in registry — return plain preformatted text
161	      const escaped = code
162	        .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
163	        .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
164	        .replace(/>/g, "&gt;");
165	      const plain = `<pre><code>${escaped}</code></pre>`;
166	      return [addPreClass(plain), addPreClass(plain)];
167	    }
168	  }
169	}
170	
171	// Create a singleton instance of the highlighter manager
172	const highlighterManager = new HighlighterManager();
173	
174	// Remove background styles from <pre> tags (inline style)
175	const removePreBackground = (html: string) => {
176	  return html.replace(
177	    /(<pre[^>]*)(style="[^"]*background[^";]*;?[^"]*")([^>]*>)/g,
178	    "$1$3"
179	  );
180	};
181	
182	// Composable CodeBlock components following artifact.tsx pattern
183	
184	export type CodeBlockProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>;
185	
186	export const CodeBlock = ({ className, ...props }: CodeBlockProps) => (
187	  <div
188	    className={cn("w-full overflow-hidden rounded-xl", className)}
189	    {...props}
190	  />
191	);
192	
193	export type CodeBlockHeaderProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & {
194	  language?: BundledLanguage;
195	};
196	
197	export const CodeBlockHeader = ({
198	  language,
199	  className,
200	  children,
201	  ...props
202	}: CodeBlockHeaderProps) => (
203	  <div
204	    className={cn(
205	      "flex items-center justify-between bg-muted/80 p-3 text-muted-foreground text-xs",
206	      className
207	    )}
208	    data-code-block-header
209	    data-language={language}
210	    {...props}
211	  >
212	    {language && <span className="ml-1 font-mono lowercase">{language}</span>}
213	    {children}
214	  </div>
215	);
216	
217	export type CodeBlockActionsProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>;
218	
219	export const CodeBlockActions = ({
220	  className,
221	  ...props
222	}: CodeBlockActionsProps) => (
223	  <div className={cn("flex items-center gap-2", className)} {...props} />
224	);
225	
226	export type CodeBlockContentProps = HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & {
227	  code: string;
228	  language: BundledLanguage;
229	  preClassName?: string;
230	};
231	
232	export const CodeBlockContent = ({
233	  code,
234	  language,
235	  preClassName,
236	  className,
237	  ...rest
238	}: CodeBlockContentProps) => {
239	  const [html, setHtml] = useState<string>("");
240	  const [darkHtml, setDarkHtml] = useState<string>("");
241	  const mounted = useRef(false);
242	  useTheme();
243	
244	  // Map next-themes values to BundledTheme
245	  const getThemes = useCallback((): [BundledTheme, BundledTheme] => {
246	    const lightTheme: BundledTheme = "github-light";
247	    const darkTheme: BundledTheme = "github-dark";
248	    return [lightTheme, darkTheme];
249	  }, []);
250	
251	  useEffect(() => {
252	    mounted.current = true;
253	    const [lightTheme, darkTheme] = getThemes();
254	
255	    void highlighterManager
256	      .highlightCode(code, language, [lightTheme, darkTheme], preClassName)
257	      .then(([light, dark]) => {
258	        if (mounted.current) {
259	          setHtml(light);
260	          setDarkHtml(dark);
261	        }
262	      });
263	
264	    return () => {
265	      mounted.current = false;
266	    };
267	  }, [code, language, preClassName, getThemes]);
268	
269	  return (
270	    <CodeBlockContext.Provider value={{ code }}>
271	      <div className="w-full border-0">
272	        <div className="min-w-full border-0">
273	          <div
274	            className={cn(
275	              "overflow-x-auto border-0 text-xs dark:hidden",
276	              // Override Shiki's default styles to remove all borders and backgrounds
277	              "[&>pre]:!border-0 [&>pre]:!bg-transparent [&>pre]:!p-0 [&>pre]:!m-0",
278	              "[&_code]:!border-0 [&_code]:!bg-transparent [&_code]:!p-0",
279	              "[&_*]:!border-0",
280	              className
281	            )}
282	            // biome-ignore lint/security/noDangerouslySetInnerHtml: Shiki syntax-highlighted HTML output
283	            dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }}
284	            data-code-block
285	            data-language={language}
286	            {...rest}
287	          />
288	          <div
289	            className={cn(
290	              "hidden overflow-x-auto border-0 text-xs dark:block",
291	              // Override Shiki's default styles to remove all borders and backgrounds
292	              "[&>pre]:!border-0 [&>pre]:!bg-transparent [&>pre]:!p-0 [&>pre]:!m-0",
293	              "[&_code]:!border-0 [&_code]:!bg-transparent [&_code]:!p-0",
294	              "[&_*]:!border-0",
295	              className
296	            )}
297	            // biome-ignore lint/security/noDangerouslySetInnerHtml: Shiki syntax-highlighted HTML output
298	            dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: darkHtml }}
299	            data-code-block
300	            data-language={language}
301	            {...rest}
302	          />
303	        </div>
304	      </div>
305	    </CodeBlockContext.Provider>
306	  );
307	};

629	export const CodeBlockDownloadButton = ({
630	  onDownload,
631	  onError,
632	  language,
633	  children,
634	  className,
635	  code: propCode,
636	  ...props
637	}: CodeBlockDownloadButtonProps & {
638	  code?: string;
639	  language?: BundledLanguage;
640	}) => {
641	  const contextCode = useContext(CodeBlockContext).code;
642	  const code = propCode ?? contextCode;
643	  const extension =
644	    language && language in languageExtensionMap
645	      ? languageExtensionMap[language]
646	      : "txt";
647	  const filename = `file.${extension}`;
648	  const mimeType = "text/plain";
649	
650	  const downloadCode = () => {
651	    try {
652	      save(filename, code, mimeType);
653	      onDownload?.();
654	    } catch (error) {
655	      onError?.(error as Error);
656	    }
657	  };
658	
659	  return (
660	    <button
661	      className={cn(
662	        "cursor-pointer p-1 text-muted-foreground transition-all hover:text-foreground",
663	        className
664	      )}
665	      onClick={downloadCode}
666	      title="Download file"
667	      type="button"
668	      {...props}
669	    >
670	      {children ?? <DownloadIcon size={14} />}
671	    </button>
672	  );
673	};
674	
675	export const CodeBlockCopyButton = ({
676	  onCopy,
677	  onError,
678	  timeout = 2000,
679	  children,
680	  className,
681	  code: propCode,
682	  ...props
683	}: CodeBlockCopyButtonProps & { code?: string }) => {
684	  const [isCopied, setIsCopied] = useState(false);
685	  const timeoutRef = useRef(0);
686	  const contextCode = useContext(CodeBlockContext).code;
687	  const code = propCode ?? contextCode;
688	
689	  const copyToClipboard = async () => {
690	    try {
691	      if (!isCopied) {
692	        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code);
693	        setIsCopied(true);
694	        onCopy?.();
695	        timeoutRef.current = window.setTimeout(
696	          () => setIsCopied(false),
697	          timeout
698	        );
699	      }
700	    } catch (error) {
701	      onError?.(error as Error);
702	    }
703	  };
704	
705	  useEffect(() => {
706	    return () => {
707	      window.clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current);
708	    };
709	  }, []);
710	
711	  const Icon = isCopied ? CheckIcon : CopyIcon;
712	
713	  return (
714	    <button
715	      className={cn(
716	        "cursor-pointer p-1 text-muted-foreground transition-all hover:text-foreground",
717	        className
718	      )}
719	      onClick={copyToClipboard}
720	      type="button"
721	      {...props}
722	    >
723	      {children ?? <Icon size={14} />}
724	    </button>
725	  );
726	};
727

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { cn } from "@repo/ui/lib/utils";
4	import type React from "react";
5	import { isValidElement, memo } from "react";
6	import type { Components as ReactMarkdownComponents } from "react-markdown";
7	import ReactMarkdown from "react-markdown";
8	import remarkGfm from "remark-gfm";
9	import type { BundledLanguage } from "shiki";
10	import {
11	  CodeBlock,
12	  CodeBlockActions,
13	  CodeBlockContent,
14	  CodeBlockCopyButton,
15	  CodeBlockHeader,
16	} from "./ai-elements/code-block";
17	
18	// Properly typed component props based on react-markdown's actual types
19	type MarkdownComponentProps = React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLElement> & {

2	import langBash from "@shikijs/langs/bash";

4	import langGo from "@shikijs/langs/go";
5	import langHtml from "@shikijs/langs/html";
6	import langJs from "@shikijs/langs/javascript";
7	import langJson from "@shikijs/langs/json";
8	import langJsonc from "@shikijs/langs/jsonc";
9	import langJsx from "@shikijs/langs/jsx";
10	import langMarkdown from "@shikijs/langs/markdown";
11	import langPy from "@shikijs/langs/python";
12	import langRust from "@shikijs/langs/rust";
13	import langSql from "@shikijs/langs/sql";
14	import langTsx from "@shikijs/langs/tsx";
15	import langTs from "@shikijs/langs/typescript";
16	import langYaml from "@shikijs/langs/yaml";
17	import githubLightDefault from "@shikijs/themes/github-light-default";
18	import { toJsxRuntime } from "hast-util-to-jsx-runtime";
19	import { Fragment } from "react";
20	import { jsx, jsxs } from "react/jsx-runtime";
21	import type { BundledLanguage } from "shiki";
22	import type { HighlighterCore } from "shiki/core";
23	import { createHighlighterCore } from "shiki/core";
24	import { createJavaScriptRegexEngine } from "shiki/engine/javascript";
25	import { SSRCodeBlockCopyButton } from "./copy-button";
26	import { openaiDark } from "./openai-dark-theme";
27	
28	// Singleton highlighter — initialized once, reused across all SSR renders
29	let _highlighterPromise: Promise<HighlighterCore> | null = null;
30	
31	function getHighlighter(): Promise<HighlighterCore> {
32	  if (!_highlighterPromise) {
33	    _highlighterPromise = createHighlighterCore({
34	      themes: [githubLightDefault, openaiDark],
35	      langs: [
36	        langTs,
37	        langJs,
38	        langTsx,
39	        langJsx,
40	        langBash,
41	        langJson,
42	        langJsonc,
43	        langYaml,
44	        langPy,
45	        langGo,
46	        langRust,
47	        langSql,
48	        langCss,
49	        langHtml,
50	        langMarkdown,
51	      ],
52	      engine: createJavaScriptRegexEngine(),
53	    });
54	  }
55	  return _highlighterPromise;
56	}
57	
58	// Re-export types for consumers
59	export type { BundledLanguage, BundledTheme } from "shiki";
60	
61	interface SSRCodeBlockProps {
62	  children: string;
63	  className?: string;
64	  language?: string;
65	  showHeader?: boolean;
66	}
67	
68	export async function SSRCodeBlock({
69	  children,
70	  language = "typescript",
71	  className,
72	  showHeader = true,
73	}: SSRCodeBlockProps) {
74	  const code = children.trim();
75	
76	  // Normalize language names - handle common aliases and invalid languages
77	  let lang: BundledLanguage = language as BundledLanguage;
78	  const languageLower = language.toLowerCase();
79	
80	  // Map common invalid/alias names to valid Shiki languages
81	  const languageMap: Record<string, BundledLanguage> = {
82	    plaintext: "plaintext" as BundledLanguage,
83	    plain: "plaintext" as BundledLanguage,
84	  };
85	
86	  const mappedLang = languageMap[languageLower];
87	  if (mappedLang) {
88	    lang = mappedLang;
89	  }
90	
91	  // Type for HAST tree nodes, extracted from toJsxRuntime's expected parameter
92	  type HastNodes = Parameters<typeof toJsxRuntime>[0];
93	
94	  // Generate HAST for both themes in parallel, with fallback to plain text
95	  const hl = await getHighlighter();
96	  let lightHast: HastNodes;
97	  let darkHast: HastNodes;
98	  try {
99	    [lightHast, darkHast] = (await Promise.all([
100	      hl.codeToHast(code, {
101	        lang,
102	        theme: "github-light-default",
103	      }),
104	      hl.codeToHast(code, {
105	        lang,
106	        theme: "openai-dark",
107	      }),
108	    ])) as [HastNodes, HastNodes];
109	  } catch {
110	    // If language is not supported, fall back to plain text
111	    [lightHast, darkHast] = (await Promise.all([
112	      hl.codeToHast(code, {
113	        lang: "text",
114	        theme: "github-light-default",
115	      }),
116	      hl.codeToHast(code, {
117	        lang: "text",
118	        theme: "openai-dark",
119	      }),
120	    ])) as [HastNodes, HastNodes];
121	  }
122	
123	  // Convert HAST to JSX for both themes
124	  // Override elements to control styling while preserving Shiki's syntax colors
125	  // IMPORTANT: leading-[1.7] must be set here directly because the className overrides parent styles
126	  const createShikiComponents = (mode: "light" | "dark") => ({
127	    pre: ({
128	      style: _style,
129	      ...props
130	    }: {
131	      style?: React.CSSProperties;
132	      [key: string]: unknown;
133	    }) => (
134	      <pre
135	        {...props}
136	        className="m-0 border-0 bg-transparent p-0 leading-[1.7]! tracking-normal"
137	      />
138	    ),
139	    code: ({
140	      style: _style,
141	      ...props
142	    }: {
143	      style?: React.CSSProperties;
144	      [key: string]: unknown;
145	    }) => <code {...props} className="block leading-[1.7]!" />,
146	    // Ensure spans have visible text color - use Shiki's color if provided, else fallback
147	    span: ({
148	      style,
149	      ...props
150	    }: {
151	      style?: React.CSSProperties;
152	      [key: string]: unknown;
153	    }) => {
154	      const colorValue = style?.color;
155	      const hasColor = colorValue && colorValue !== "inherit";
156	      const fallbackColor = mode === "light" ? "#24292e" : "#dcdcdc";
157	      return (
158	        <span
159	          {...props}
160	          className="leading-[1.7]!"
161	          style={{
162	            ...style,
163	            color: hasColor ? colorValue : fallbackColor,
164	            letterSpacing: "normal",
165	          }}
166	        />
167	      );
168	    },
169	  });
170	
171	  function hastToJsx(hast: HastNodes, mode: "light" | "dark"): React.ReactNode {
172	    // toJsxRuntime types depend on the `hast` module which eslint's type checker
173	    // cannot fully resolve, requiring the explicit cast here
174	    return toJsxRuntime(hast, {
175	      Fragment,
176	      jsx,
177	      jsxs,
178	      components: createShikiComponents(mode),
179	    } as Parameters<typeof toJsxRuntime>[1]) as React.ReactNode;
180	  }
181	
182	  const lightJsx = hastToJsx(lightHast, "light");
183	  const darkJsx = hastToJsx(darkHast, "dark");
184	
185	  return (
186	    <div className={cn("my-4", className)}>
187	      <div className="scrollbar-thin overflow-hidden rounded-sm dark:bg-card/80">
188	        {/* Header with language label and copy button */}
189	        {showHeader && (
190	          <div className="flex items-center justify-between py-2 pr-3 pl-6">
191	            <span className="font-medium font-mono text-muted-foreground text-xs">
192	              {language}
193	            </span>
194	            <SSRCodeBlockCopyButton code={code} />
195	          </div>
196	        )}
197	
198	        {/* Code content with line numbers */}
199	        <div className="overflow-x-auto px-6 pb-4">
200	          <div className="flex min-w-max">
201	            {/* Code content - dual theme rendering */}
202	            {/* text-foreground provides base color for plain text (no syntax highlighting) */}
203	            <div className="flex-1 text-foreground">
204	              {/* Light theme - hidden in dark mode */}
205	              <div className="block font-mono text-xs dark:hidden">
206	                {lightJsx}
207	              </div>
208	              {/* Dark theme - hidden in light mode */}
209	              <div className="hidden font-mono text-sm dark:block">
210	                {darkJsx}
211	              </div>
212	            </div>
213	          </div>
214	        </div>
215	      </div>
216	    </div>
217	  );
218	}
219	
220	// Re-export the copy button for consumers who need it separately
221	export { SSRCodeBlockCopyButton } from "./copy-button";
222

Consumer files otherwise typecheck clean against 4.x; no `createHighlighter` / `codeToHtml` / `bundledLanguages` API regressions surfaced.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 8: lucide-react 1.x"

1	"use client";
2	
3	import { Button } from "@repo/ui/components/ui/button";
4	import { Link2, Linkedin, Twitter } from "lucide-react";
5	import { useState } from "react";
6	
7	interface SocialShareProps {
8	  description?: string;
9	  title: string;
10	  url: string;
11	}
12	
13	export function SocialShare({
14	  title,
15	  url,
16	  description: _description,
17	}: SocialShareProps) {
18	  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
19	
20	  const shareLinks = {
21	    twitter: `https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?text=${encodeURIComponent(
22	      title
23	    )}&url=${encodeURIComponent(url)}`,
24	    linkedin: `https://www.linkedin.com/sharing/share-offsite/?url=${encodeURIComponent(
25	      url
26	    )}`,
27	  };
28	
29	  const handleCopyLink = async () => {
30	    try {
31	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
32	      setCopied(true);
33	      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
34	    } catch (err) {
35	      console.error("Failed to copy:", err);
36	    }
37	  };
38	
39	  return (
40	    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
41	      <span className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Share:</span>
42	
43	      <Button
44	        aria-label="Share on Twitter"
45	        className="h-6 w-6"
46	        onClick={() => window.open(shareLinks.twitter, "_blank")}
47	        size="icon"
48	        variant="ghost"
49	      >
50	        <Twitter className="size-3.5" />
51	      </Button>
52	
53	      <Button
54	        aria-label="Share on LinkedIn"
55	        className="h-6 w-6"
56	        onClick={() => window.open(shareLinks.linkedin, "_blank")}
57	        size="icon"
58	        variant="ghost"
59	      >
60	        <Linkedin className="size-3.5" />
61	      </Button>
62	
63	      <Button
64	        aria-label="Copy link"
65	        className="h-6 w-6"
66	        onClick={handleCopyLink}
67	        size="icon"
68	        variant="ghost"
69	      >
70	        <Link2 className="size-3.5" />
71	        {copied && <span className="ml-2 text-green-600 text-xs">Copied!</span>}
72	      </Button>
73	    </div>
74	  );
75	}
76
